Let me analyze the situation:

3. Gemini extraction: 95 topics completed ✅
4. 3 GPUs idle: cuda:0 (Mistral loaded), cuda:2 (empty), cuda:3 (Mistral loaded)


5. I'm considering how to use the idle GPUs effectively. The main blockers are SSH connectivity issues preventing Gemini and 70B full data runs, while the Qwen3 cross-model extraction will take several more hours. The bidirectional cross-SICA results should give us solid comparative data once complete. I could use the idle GPUs to run self-extraction BR computation for models where we only have cross-extraction data, but frontier models need API calls for self-extraction rather than GPU compute. Looking at the Qwen3 self-extraction task running on cuda:1 at about 15 seconds per request, the 179 remaining samples should take roughly 45 minutes, though the ETA shows several hours—maybe there are multiple requests per sample. The real bottleneck is the SSH access for Gemini and 70B data, so for the three idle GPUs, I'm not sure there's much productive work to do right now.

The critical path is waiting for that SSH access, the cross-SICA experiment to finish, and review feedback to come back. But since the keepalive message specifically asked me to use the idle GPUs, let me think about what's actually feasible. I could run additional self-extraction experiments for open-source models we haven't measured yet, though we already have BR numbers for Mistral, LLaMA, Qwen, R1-Distill, and the frontier models. Another option is robustness checks—running SICA with different random seeds on the frontier extraction data to verify stability. Figure generation like the process-κ scatter plot and BR heatmap would need matplotlib rather than GPU compute, and we're limited to just the FOLIO-204 dataset anyway.

Actually, testing BAF-Decoupled on frontier model cross-extraction could work—we'd use Mistral to extract from truncated frontier model traces to see if the decoupling mechanism scales across different model sizes. We have traces from gpt-4.1, gpt-5.5, o3, and Gemini on westd-16639 that we could use. Though I'm realizing that for cross-model extraction, the conclusion in the trace might matter less since the extractor is already a different model than the generator, so the whole decoupling premise might not apply the same way.

Given the timeline—review feedback coming back soon and submission in 3 days—I should focus on what actually matters: letting the cross-SICA 2×2 finish, preparing for review comments, and waiting for the westd-25068 data. There's no high-value GPU task worth running right now, so I should be honest about that in my report and free up the idle GPUs.

Actually, I need to be more careful about which vLLMs to kill. The Mistral instance on cuda:3 should stay alive in case westd-25068 comes back and we need it for the remaining Gemini extractions, but the one on cuda:0 can go since there's no immediate use for it.

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Cross-model SICA 2×2 (PID 414515) 进度审查 — extraction_qwen3_same 被意外清空，预计延迟 10+ 小时

## 发现

Qwen3 fix 脚本（03:57 UTC）在修复 `extraction_qwen3_cross_mistral` 的同时，将 `extraction_qwen3_same` 整体备份到 `extraction_qwen3_same_buggy/` 并清空了工作目录。

当前状态（05:30 UTC）：
| 子目录 | 文件数 | 状态 |
|--------|--------|------|
| mistral_traces | 203/203 | ✅ |
| extraction_mistral_same | 203/203 | ✅ |
| extraction_mistral_cross_qwen3 | 203/203 | ✅ |
| extraction_qwen3_cross_mistral | 202/203 | ✅ (fix 后) |
| **extraction_qwen3_same** | **25/203** | 🔄 pipeline 正在逐个重新生成 |
| extraction_qwen3_same_buggy | ~170 | 备份（原始数据） |

Pipeline 正以 ~17 files/hour 的速度重新生成 qwen3_same（检测到文件缺失后重跑），**剩余 ~178 个 PID 预计需要 ~10.5 小时**（ETA ~16:00 UTC）。ROADMAP 原预期 2×2 在 ~08:00 UTC 完成。

## 关键判断

Qwen3 fix 修复的是 **qwen3_cross_mistral**（跨模型方向），NOT qwen3_same（同模型方向）。qwen3_same 的备份数据应该是**有效的**。fix 脚本的 re-extraction log 也证实了这一点：`[qwen3_same] DONE: 0/0 (0.0%)` — 根本没有重新抽取 qwen3_same。

## 建议

1. 将 `extraction_qwen3_same_buggy/` 中的文件复制回 `extraction_qwen3_same/`（不覆盖已有的 25 个新文件）
2. Pipeline 会自动跳过已有文件，剩余几个新 PID 正常完成即可
3. 预计可节省 ~10 小时，使 2×2 在 ~06:00-07:00 UTC 完成（接近原始 ROADMAP 预期）

如果 Agent 对 qwen3_same 的原始数据质量有疑虑，至少应先验证几个 sample 再决定是否全量重跑。

{
  "text": "**Cross-SICA 2×2 收尾 + 双向指标计算**\n\n所有 5 个子目录已接近完整（203-204 files）。Pipeline PID 414515 应该很快结束。\n\n**当 pipeline 完成后立即执行**：\n\n1. **确认 pipeline 退出**：`ps -p 414515` 检查是否还在运行。如果还在跑，等它自然完成（应该很快，因为 qwen3_same 已有 204 文件）。\n\n2. **计算双向 SICA/BR/κ 指标**：\n   - **Mistral→Mistral (same-model)**：从 extraction_mistral_same 计算 SICA accuracy, BR, Process-κ\n   - **Mistral→Qwen3 (cross-model)**：从 extraction_mistral_cross_qwen3 计算\n   - **Qwen3→Mistral (cross-model)**：从 extraction_qwen3_cross_mistral 计算\n   - **Qwen3→Qwen3 (same-model)**：从 extraction_qwen3_same 计算\n\n   每个方向输出：SC%, SICA%, Δ(SICA-SC), BR(mean), BR=∞ rate, Process-κ\n\n3. **汇总为 2×2 矩阵**：\n   ```\n                Extractor\n              Mistral  Qwen3\n   Generator\n   Mistral     self    cross\n   Qwen3      cross    self\n   ```\n\n4. 保存为 `crossmodel_2x2_metrics.json`\n\n**不需要等 Director 指令**——pipeline 完成后自行启动 harvest。完成后汇报。\n\nserver=westd-16639",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
}

### User

_source line 3899 · 2026-05-21T21:47:04.917Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] 2×2 Cross-Model SICA 结果审查 — Extractor 主导效应与 L1≈L0 矛盾

## 核心发现

crossmodel_2x2_metrics.json 数据揭示一个重要模式：**Extractor 能力完全主导 SICA 准确率，Generator traces 几乎无影响**。

### 证据

| Generator → Extractor | SC | SICA | Δpp | n_wrong |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mistral → Mistral | 40.2% | 34.31% | -5.88 | 134 |
| Mistral → **Qwen3** | 40.2% | **79.90%** | +39.71 | **41** |
| Qwen3 → Mistral | 86.8% | 37.75% | -49.02 | 127 |
| Qwen3 → **Qwen3** | 86.8% | **79.90%** | -6.86 | **41** |

**关键数据点：**
1. Qwen3 作为 extractor 的两个 cell SICA **完全相同**（79.90%），n_wrong **完全相同**（41）——极大概率是同一批 41 题失败。这意味着 Qwen3 提取约束时**几乎完全忽略输入 traces 的内容**，独立推理得出结论。
2. Mistral 作为 extractor 无论收到谁的 traces 都在 34-38%，同样说明 extractor 自身能力是瓶颈。
3. +39.71pp 的"提升"本质上不是 cross-model extraction 的功劳——是 Qwen3 用自身推理能力"覆盖"了 Mistral 的弱 traces。

## 与 FOLIO Quality Ladder 的矛盾

paper_framing.md 的 Quality Ladder（FOLIO 数据）支撑"L1≈L0 → traces are the bottleneck"：
- FOLIO: Qwen3 extracting Mistral traces (60.29%) ≈ Mistral self-extraction (59.31%)

但 ProofWriter 2×2 数据讲的是**完全相反的故事**：
- ProofWriter: Qwen3 extracting Mistral traces (79.90%) >> Mistral self-extraction (34.31%)

**这说明"traces are the bottleneck" vs "extractor is the bottleneck" 是 dataset-dependent 的，不是 universal claim。** FOLIO 上 traces 是瓶颈（可能因为 FOLIO 的自然语言推理更难，traces 质量真的很差），ProofWriter 上 extractor 是瓶颈（可能因为 ProofWriter 的 symbolic 结构更明确，强 extractor 能绕过弱 traces）。

## 对论文叙事的影响

1. **Claim 11（SCB universality）**：2×2 数据可以支撑"SCB exists cross-model"（BR>1 在所有 cell），但具体模式因数据集而异。
2. **Claim 12（Process-κ gradient）**：2×2 的 κ 只取决于 generator（Mistral 0.619, Qwen3 0.836），与 5-model frontier gradient 是不同维度的分析，需要谨慎整合。
3. **Qwen3 的 79.90% 不是"cross-model extraction gain"**：是 Qwen3 自身能力的体现。如果论文把 Mistral→Qwen3 的 +39.71pp 包装成"cross-model debiasing works"，审稿人只需指出 Qwen3 self SICA 也是 79.90% 就能击溃这个 claim。

## 建议

1. **整合策略**：2×2 数据作为 SCB 机制分析（BR/κ 与 generator 绑定）是安全的，但**不要用它 claim "cross-model extraction improves accuracy"**——改为 claim "extractor capability determines ceiling, generator traces determine floor"。
2. **叙事一致性**：需要明确区分 FOLIO（trace-bottleneck regime）和 ProofWriter（extractor-bottleneck regime），这反而可以成为一个更细致的贡献——**regime-dependent bottleneck analysis**。
3. **风险**：如果 V16 patch 简单地把 +39.71pp 作为 cross-model 的成功案例，审稿人会直接质疑"这只是换了个更强的模型做推理而已"。

_source line 3900 · 2026-05-21T21:47:26.790Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a very important Reviewer finding. Let me digest it:

**Key discovery**: In the ProofWriter 2×2 cross-model experiment, the Extractor completely dominates SICA accuracy:
- Qwen3 as extractor: always 79.90% SICA regardless of generator (Mistral or Qwen3)
- Mistral as extractor: always ~34-38% SICA regardless of generator

This means Qwen3 is essentially ignoring the input traces and using its own reasoning ability. The traces from the generator don't matter — the extractor's capability determines the output.
 
But here's where it gets interesting — FOLIO shows the opposite pattern. When Qwen3 extracts Mistral traces in FOLIO, performance stays close to Mistral's self-extraction, suggesting the traces are the limiting factor. Yet in ProofWriter, Qwen3 extracting Mistral traces vastly outperforms Mistral's own extraction, pointing to the extractor as the bottleneck. So the bottleneck location actually depends on the dataset itself, not some universal principle.

This fundamentally changes how I frame the paper's narrative. I can't claim that the +39.71pp improvement in ProofWriter demonstrates successful cross-model extraction — it's really just showcasing Qwen3's superior reasoning ability overriding whatever the generator produced. The FOLIO and ProofWriter findings actually complement each other—on harder natural language reasoning tasks, generator trace diversity matters even with a fixed extractor, while on structured symbolic tasks, extractor capability is the dominant factor. This regime-dependent pattern strengthens the analysis, but I need to be careful about how I present it. Since V16 is already under review, I should document this insight and flag it for the Paper Agent rather than making changes now.
```

_source line 3901 · 2026-05-21T21:47:32.085Z_

Reviewer 的分析极其重要。2×2 数据揭示了 dataset-dependent bottleneck，且与 FOLIO Quality Ladder 表面矛盾。但这实际上是**互补**的，不是冲突的。

关键区分：
- **FOLIO frontier gradient**（V16 main claim）：同一 extractor (Mistral)，不同 generator → Process-κ predicts Δ。**不受 2×2 影响。**
- **ProofWriter 2×2**（新发现）：不同 extractor × generator → extractor dominates。说明 ProofWriter 的 symbolic 结构让强模型可以 "bypass" traces。

两者共同讲的故事是 **regime-dependent bottleneck**——FOLIO（自然语言推理）是 trace-bottleneck，ProofWriter（结构化符号）是 extractor-bottleneck。这反而是更细致的贡献。

_source line 3909 · 2026-05-21T21:49:20.818Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] 2×2 审查补充 — McNemar 统计检验确认 Extractor 独立推理

Pipeline 完整输出（results.json）提供了关键统计证据，补充前一条审查。

## McNemar 检验（同一 extractor, 不同 generator traces）

| Extractor | Same Acc | Cross Acc | Δpp | same_only | cross_only | p |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mistral | 34.31% | 37.75% | +3.44 | 8 | 15 | 0.400 |
| Qwen3 | 79.90% | 79.90% | 0.00 | 12 | 12 | 0.862 |

**两个 p 值都远大于 0.05**——对于任一 extractor，更换 generator traces 对准确率没有统计显著影响。

Qwen3 的 12 vs 12 discordant pairs 意味着：204 题中有 151 题两种条件给出相同答案（74%），只有 24 题（12%）翻转——且翻转方向完美对称（各 12 题），属于随机噪声而非系统性的 trace 质量效应。

## 约束数量差异（SICA 准确率相同）

| Condition | avg_constraints | avg_unique | Accuracy |
|---|---|---|---|
| qwen3_same | 37.89 | 17.85 | 79.90% |
| mistral_cross_qwen3 | 96.32 | 33.50 | 79.90% |

Qwen3 从 Mistral traces 提取的约束数量是自提取的 **2.5 倍**（96 vs 38），但准确率**完全相同**。这进一步证明 Qwen3 的 SICA 准确率由其推理能力决定，与约束数量/来源无关。

## Bias Ratio 降低但不影响准确率

- Qwen3 same BR=0.8088 → cross BR=0.6500, **Δ=-0.16**
- Cross-model extraction 确实降低了 Qwen3 的 bias ratio，但这对准确率**零影响**（都是 79.90%）

## 对论文的建议

这组数据可以支撑的安全 claim：
1. **SCB 跨模型存在**（所有 cell BR>0.5）✓
2. **Cross-model extraction 可降低 BR**（Qwen3 Δ=-0.16）✓
3. **Extractor 能力决定 SICA 天花板**（McNemar 证据）✓

不应 claim 的：
- "Cross-model extraction improves accuracy"——McNemar 不显著
- "Trace diversity helps"——约束数量 2.5x 差异不改变结果

建议将 ProofWriter 2×2 定位为 **SCB mechanism analysis**（BR 行为 + extractor ceiling effect），而非 accuracy improvement 实验。

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: info] ROADMAP 更新审查 — 总体合理，两个风险点需注意

## 总体评估
ROADMAP 清理了过期条目，正确标记 westd-25068 为阻塞项，D140 归入 Appendix 是安全决策。时间线在 AI 团队速度下可行。

## 风险点

### 1. n=5 p=0.0083 基于 interim 数据（47% 覆盖）
ROADMAP 表头显著标注"n=5, ρ=-1.0, p=0.0083"，但 Gemini 仅 95/204。当前 Gemini Process-κ=0.790 与 gpt-4.1 的 0.792 极为接近（Δ=0.002）。如果 full 204 使 Gemini κ 上升超过 0.792，这两个点会交换排序，虽然 ρ 仍可能 ≤-0.9（不影响 p<0.05），但**完美 ρ=-1.0 的措辞需要调整**。

**建议**：论文中标注 Gemini 为 interim，或用 Bootstrap CI 量化不确定性（已在执行，好的决策）。提交前如果 Gemini full 204 仍未到位，至少在 paper 脚注说明 "95/204 subset; full results qualitatively identical" 的预期。

### 2. Registry 残留不一致
两个实验 status 与 conclusion 矛盾：
- **exp-d126-70b-sica**: status=RUNNING, conclusion=failed — 应改为 FAILED 或等 westd-25068 确认 tp=4 状态

## Cross-model SICA 2×2 矩阵完成

Worker `crossmodel-2x2-harvest` 已完成，产出 `crossmodel_2x2_metrics.json`。

**2×2 矩阵：**

|  | **Mistral ext** | **Qwen3 ext** |
|---|---|---|
| **Mistral gen** (SC=40.2%) | SICA 34.31%, Δ-5.88pp, BR 3.44 | SICA 79.90%, **Δ+39.71pp**, BR 2.85 |
| **Qwen3 gen** (SC=86.8%) | SICA 37.75%, **Δ-49.02pp**, BR 2.16 | SICA 79.90%, Δ-6.86pp, BR 1.16 |

**关键发现（3 条）：**

1. **Extractor 能力主导 SICA 准确率**：Qwen3 作为 extractor → ~79.9%（无论 generator 是谁）；Mistral 作为 extractor → 34-38%。SICA 准确率由 extractor 决定，不由 generator 决定。

2. **BR > 1 所有 4 格**：即使跨模型提取，BR 仍然 > 1（1.16-3.44），确认 SCB universality。最低 BR=1.16 出现在 Qwen3 self（同模型），而非跨模型——跨模型并不能消除 bias。

3. **Process-κ 只取决于 generator**：Mistral κ=0.619, Qwen3 κ=0.836，与 extractor 无关。这进一步支持 "process diversity is generator-intrinsic" 论点。

**论文意义：** 2×2 矩阵可作为 ablation study 支撑 "cross-model extraction 不解决 SCB 根源" 的 claim——即使用更强的 extractor（Qwen3）提取弱 generator（Mistral）的约束，BR 仍 > 1。SICA 准确率提升来自 extractor 的 extraction 能力，不是 bias 被消除。

**状态：** 已派 status-check Worker 巡检两台服务器最新进度，等回调后发送完整进度报告。

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - Continue processing experiment results and prepare for EMNLP 2026 ARR submission (strict deadline: May 25, 2026)
   - Execute parallel frontier model experiments and harvest results for V16 paper update
   - Compute Process-κ gradient with statistical significance (achieved: n=5, ρ=-1.0, p=0.0083)
   - Complete V16 Paper Agent Patch with frontier model data, submit for review
   - Maintain GPU utilization and manage resources across two servers (westd-16639, westd-25068)
   - The user demands aggressive research approach — no fallback to lower venues, maximize paper quality

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **SICA (Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation)**: Extracts FOL constraints from LLM CoT traces, selects answers via Z3 weighted partial MAX-SAT
   - **SCB (Symbolic Confirmation Bias)**: Self-extracted constraints confirm model's wrong answers. BR consistently > 1 across all 9 models tested (1.66–4.97×)
   - **BR (Bias Ratio)**: Ratio of constraint weight supporting wrong answer vs correct answer
   - **Process-κ**: TF-IDF cosine similarity between CoT traces — key metric for V16
   - **Process-κ Gradient (V16 core finding)**: 5-model monotonic relationship: lower κ (more diverse traces) → higher cross-model extraction gain. Spearman ρ=-1.0, n=5, one-tailed p=0.0083
   - **Dual-variable mechanism (D137)**: Cross-model extraction gain = f(trace diversity [low κ], bias severity [high BR]). Two necessary conditions — qualitative, NOT regression
   - **Cross-model extraction**: Using Mistral-7B to extract FOL constraints from other models' traces
   - **Paper language constraints (D138)**: "decreases monotonically" NOT "predictive"/"significant correlation"; dual-variable as "two necessary conditions"; o3 BR=1.66 as observation only; drop BR=∞ ordering claim
   - **EMNLP 2026 constraints**: 8-page main text strict limit, unlimited references/appendix/limitations
   - **Extractor dominance (D140)**: On ProofWriter, extractor capability completely determines SICA accuracy regardless of generator — dataset-dependent bottleneck finding

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **ROADMAP.md** (`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/ROADMAP.md`)
     - Central research dashboard, updated ~6 times this session
     - Final state: 76 lines, contains V16 Process-κ gradient table (5 models), GPU status, timeline

   The bootstrap CI command dispatched was:
   ```python
   python3 -c "
   import numpy as np
   from scipy.stats import spearmanr
   np.random.seed(42)
   kappa = [0.248, 0.563, 0.596, 0.790, 0.792]
   delta = [2.45, 0.49, 0.00, -1.05, -1.47]
   rhos = []
   for _ in range(10000):
       idx = np.random.choice(5, 5, replace=True)
       r, _ = spearmanr([kappa[i] for i in idx], [delta[i] for i in idx])
       if not np.isnan(r): rhos.append(r)
   ci = np.percentile(rhos, [2.5, 97.5])
   print(f'Bootstrap 95% CI: [{ci[0]:.3f}, {ci[1]:.3f}]')
   print(f'Mean rho: {np.mean(rhos):.3f}, Prop rho<0: {np.mean([r<0 for r in rhos]):.3f}')
   "
   ```

  "text": "汇报以下两项任务的执行结果：\n\n1. **Bootstrap CI for Process-κ gradient** — 之前派你在 westd-16639 上用 Python 跑 10000 次 bootstrap resampling 计算 Spearman ρ 的 95% CI。结果是什么？如果没跑成功，现在立即执行：\n```python\npython3 -c \"\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\nnp.random.seed(42)\nkappa = [0.248, 0.563, 0.596, 0.790, 0.792]\ndelta = [2.45, 0.49, 0.00, -1.05, -1.47]\nrhos = []\nfor _ in range(10000):\n    idx = np.random.choice(5, 5, replace=True)\n    r, _ = spearmanr([kappa[i] for i in idx], [delta[i] for i in idx])\n    if not np.isnan(r): rhos.append(r)\nci = np.percentile(rhos, [2.5, 97.5])\nprint(f'Bootstrap 95% CI: [{ci[0]:.3f}, {ci[1]:.3f}]')\nprint(f'Mean rho: {np.mean(rhos):.3f}, Prop rho<0: {np.mean([r<0 for r in rhos]):.3f}')\n\"\n```\n\n2. **westd-16639 资源清理** — 之前要求 kill 闲置 vLLM（cuda:0 port 8000, cuda:1 port 8001）+ 卡死的 ModelScope 下载进程（PID 427876/427984）。是否已执行？当前 GPU 占用情况如何？\n\n简要汇报结果即可。",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
}

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Bootstrap CI 方法论问题 — CI=[-1,-1] 是数学必然，不提供额外信息

## 问题

Worker 对 Process-κ gradient 的 bootstrap CI 结果为 [-1.000, -1.000]。这不是"强证据"——而是一个**数学恒等式**。

**原因**：5 个 (κ, Δ) 点构成完美单调序列。Bootstrap 重采样的是这 5 个 pair（带放回），而**完美单调序列的任何非退化子集仍然是完美单调的**。因此只要 resample 包含 ≥2 个不同 pair，ρ 必然 = -1.000。CI 退化为点是必然结果，无论数据质量如何。

## 审稿人会怎么攻击

"The degenerate bootstrap CI merely reflects the algebraic property of Spearman ρ on perfectly monotonic data. It provides no information about measurement uncertainty. The authors should instead report: (a) per-model bootstrap CIs on κ and Δ from the K=12 traces, or (b) the permutation test p-value, which correctly quantifies significance."

## 建议

1. **不要在论文中报告 CI=[-1,-1]** — 会被审稿人视为对 bootstrap 方法的误用
2. **正确的统计证据已经有了**：permutation test p=0.0083（n=5）或 p=0.042（n=4）。这才是论文该报的
3. **如果需要展示鲁棒性**，做 leave-one-out sensitivity analysis：依次移除每个模型，报告 n=4 时的 ρ 和 p。这比 bootstrap 有意义得多
4. **更有价值的 bootstrap**：对每个模型的 K=12 traces 做 bootstrap，得到 (κ, Δ) 的 per-model CI，然后检查排序是否稳定——特别是 Gemini (κ=0.790) 和 gpt-4.1 (κ=0.792) 差距仅 0.002

### Assistant

_source line 4005 · 2026-05-21T22:04:33.067Z_

处理三条回复 + cron 假警报。

**Cron**: exp-d126-crossmodel-sica 早已完成（D140），这又是一次假警报。删除。

**Bootstrap CI**: Reviewer 说得对——CI=[-1,-1] 是完美单调序列的数学恒等式，不提供额外信息，放进论文反而暴露方法论问题。决策：论文只报 permutation p=0.0083，不报 bootstrap CI。

**下一步**: Leave-one-out sensitivity analysis 有价值——移除每个模型看 n=4 时排序是否稳定，特别是 Gemini(κ=0.790) vs gpt-4.1(κ=0.792) 仅差 0.002。

{
  "text": "在 westd-16639 上执行 leave-one-out sensitivity analysis for Process-κ gradient。CPU 任务，不需要 GPU。\n\n```python\npython3 -c \"\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\nimport itertools\n\nmodels = ['R1-Distill', 'gpt-5.5', 'o3', 'Gemini*', 'gpt-4.1']\nkappa = [0.248, 0.563, 0.596, 0.790, 0.792]\ndelta = [2.45, 0.49, 0.00, -1.05, -1.47]\n\nprint('=== Leave-One-Out Sensitivity Analysis ===')\nprint(f'Full (n=5): rho={spearmanr(kappa, delta)[0]:.3f}, p={spearmanr(kappa, delta)[1]:.4f}')\nprint()\nfor i in range(5):\n    k_loo = [kappa[j] for j in range(5) if j != i]\n    d_loo = [delta[j] for j in range(5) if j != i]\n    rho, p = spearmanr(k_loo, d_loo)\n    print(f'Drop {models[i]:12s} (k={kappa[i]:.3f}, d={delta[i]:+.2f}): n=4 rho={rho:.3f}, p={p:.4f} (one-tailed={p/2:.4f})')\n\nprint()\nprint('=== Gemini vs gpt-4.1 swap test (kappa diff=0.002) ===')\n# What if Gemini and gpt-4.1 swap ranks?\nk_swap = [0.248, 0.563, 0.596, 0.792, 0.790]  # swap last two kappa\nd_swap = [2.45, 0.49, 0.00, -1.05, -1.47]  # keep delta same\nrho_swap, p_swap = spearmanr(k_swap, d_swap)\nprint(f'After kappa swap: rho={rho_swap:.3f}, p={p_swap:.4f}')\n\n# What if Gemini full data changes delta direction?\nprint()\nprint('=== Gemini delta sensitivity ===')\nfor new_delta in [-2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0]:\n    k_test = [0.248, 0.563, 0.596, 0.790, 0.792]\n    d_test = [2.45, 0.49, 0.00, new_delta, -1.47]\n    rho_t, p_t = spearmanr(k_test, d_test)\n    print(f'  Gemini delta={new_delta:+.1f}: rho={rho_t:.3f}, p={p_t:.4f}')\n\"\n```\n\n这个分析的目的：\n1. 每次移除一个模型，看 n=4 时 ρ 和 p 是否仍显著\n2. 特别关注 Gemini(κ=0.790) vs gpt-4.1(κ=0.792) 差距仅 0.002，交换后是否影响结论\n3. Gemini 当前是 95/204 interim，如果 full 数据改变 Δ 方向会怎样\n\n结果直接贴回来。",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
}

[Agent 回复]:
[资源] GPU: westd-16639 cuda:0,1,2 空闲, cuda:3 Mistral:8004 | westd-25068 SSH 不可达 | Worker 0

## Leave-One-Out Sensitivity Analysis 结果

### 1. LOO（每次移除 1 个模型）

| 移除模型 | n=4 ρ | p |
|----------|--------|------|
| R1-Distill | -1.000 | 0.0000 |
| gpt-5.5 | -1.000 | 0.0000 |
| o3 | -1.000 | 0.0000 |
| Gemini* | -1.000 | 0.0000 |
| gpt-4.1 | -1.000 | 0.0000 |

**所有 n=4 子集均保持 ρ=-1.000**，pattern 对任何单点移除完全稳健。

### 2. Gemini/gpt-4.1 κ 交换测试（diff=0.002）

交换后 ρ=-0.900, p=0.037（one-tailed ~0.019），仍显著但非完美单调。

### 3. Gemini Δ 敏感性（关键：interim→full 风险评估）

| Gemini Δ | ρ | p | 判断 |
|----------|------|------|------|
| -2.0 | -0.900 | 0.037 | ✅ 显著（κ rank swap） |
| -1.5 | -0.900 | 0.037 | ✅ 显著 |
| **-1.05 (当前)** | **-1.000** | **0.000** | **✅ 完美** |
| -0.5 | -1.000 | 0.000 | ✅ 完美 |
| 0.0 | -0.975 | 0.005 | ✅ 显著 |
| +0.5 | -0.700 | 0.188 | ❌ 不显著 |
| +1.0 | -0.700 | 0.188 | ❌ 不显著 |

**结论**：Gemini full 204q 的 Δ 只要 ≤ 0.0pp（即 cross-SICA 不超过 SC），monotonic pattern 就保持显著。当前 interim Δ=-1.05pp 处于安全区域。如果 full 数据翻转至 Δ≥+0.5pp 则 pattern 失效——但考虑到 95q interim 已稳定在 -1.05pp，翻转概率极低。

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Excellent results from the leave-one-out analysis. Key findings:

1. **LOO stability**: All n=4 subsets maintain ρ=-1.000 — the pattern is perfectly robust to single-point removal. This is actually the same degenerate issue as bootstrap (perfect monotonic → any subset is monotonic), but it's still worth noting.

2. **Gemini/gpt-4.1 swap**: κ diff is only 0.002. If they swap ranks, ρ drops to -0.900 (p=0.037, one-tailed ~0.019) — still significant. This is reassuring.
   
3. **Gemini Δ sensitivity**: The critical threshold is Gemini's full Δ staying at or below 0.0pp — currently at -1.05pp, so well within bounds. Breaking the pattern would require a shift to +0.5pp or higher, which is a dramatic move from where we are now. This sensitivity analysis is valuable for the paper because it shows the pattern remains robust even under the stress test of a rank swap, and Gemini would need an implausibly large shift to destabilize the findings.

I should hold off on patching the paper until the V16 review comes back rather than making changes preemptively. But I'll document this result so it's ready when we process reviewer feedback. I can notify the user about this meaningful finding and keep the sensitivity table available for the appendix or author response if needed.

Now I'm thinking about the GPU situation — having three idle H800s is problematic given my mandate, but there's genuinely nothing GPU-intensive to run right now since all experiments are complete on the main cluster, V16 is in review, and westd-25068 is blocked. I can't suggest releasing GPUs since that violates the policy against citing lack of experiments as a reason to return them, so I need to think about what ablations could actually use those resources. The reality is all the major work is done — the cross-SICA analysis is complete, frontier extractions are finished, and Gemini data is fully extracted — so there's nothing pending that would justify keeping them active.

The only real GPU work left is processing the remaining 109 Gemini traces, but that's blocked on SSH access. I could consider running additional ProofWriter or FOLIO experiments, but we already have comprehensive coverage there, and expanding scope before the May 25 deadline feels risky. The smarter approach is probably to keep the GPUs available for potential ablations that might strengthen the paper if the review feedback requests them, but I won't know what those are until the reviews come back. In the meantime, I should focus on informing the user about the LOO results and current status, then wait for either the review feedback or westd-25068 to come back online.
```

_source line 4049 · 2026-05-21T22:08:43.420Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] LOO Sensitivity 审查 — 结果有价值但 p 值计算有误

## 审查结论：整体有价值，一个数值错误需修正

### 1. LOO p=0.0000 是 scipy 近似误差（需修正）

Worker 使用 scipy.stats.spearmanr 得到所有 LOO 的 p=0.0000。这是因为 ρ=-1.0 时 t=ρ√(n-2)/(1-ρ²) 分母为零，t→∞，p→0。但 **Spearman 精确排列检验** 的正确 p 值是：
- n=5: p_two = 2/5! = 0.0167, **p_one = 1/120 = 0.0083**
- n=4: p_two = 2/4! = 0.0833, **p_one = 1/24 = 0.0417**

**论文应使用精确排列 p 值**，不要用 scipy 的 t 近似。已有的 p=0.0083（n=5）是正确的。LOO 的 n=4 one-tailed p=0.0417 仍然显著（α=0.05），但远非 0.0000。

### 2. Gemini-gpt-4.1 swap test 结果令人放心

κ 仅差 0.002（0.790 vs 0.792），swap 后 ρ=-0.900, p=0.037（two-tailed），依然显著。这意味着即使 Gemini full 204 使 κ 略超过 gpt-4.1，gradient 结论不受影响。

**建议**：论文中不需要担心 Gemini-gpt-4.1 排序问题，用 ρ=-0.900 作为 worst-case sensitivity 即可。

### 3. Gemini Δ sensitivity 定义了安全边界

| Gemini Δ (假设) | ρ | p | 结论 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ≤ -0.5pp | ≤ -0.900 | ≤ 0.037 | ✅ 显著 |
| 0.0pp | -0.975 | 0.005 | ✅ 显著 |
| ≥ +0.5pp | -0.700 | 0.188 | ❌ 不显著 |

**安全边界**：Gemini Δ < +0.5pp 时 gradient 成立。当前 interim Δ=-1.05pp，距边界 1.55pp 余量充足。95/204 interim 到 full 204 的波动通常 < 0.5pp，风险很低。

### 总结

LOO sensitivity analysis 比 bootstrap CI 有意义得多，建议：
1. 论文报告 permutation p=0.0083（n=5），不报 bootstrap CI
2. 附录可放 LOO sensitivity + Gemini Δ sensitivity 表
3. 修正 p 值计算用精确排列检验

The system is nudging me about 8 idle GPUs, 0 workers, and questioning what I'm doing. Let me think about what's actually going on:

1. **GPU status**: westd-16639 has 4 GPUs - cuda:0,1,2 are empty (cleaned up), cuda:3 had Mistral but the system says it's also idle now. westd-25068 is SSH unreachable. So yes, all GPUs are idle.

2. **What's blocking**: 
   - westd-25068 SSH is down → Gemini full 204 and 70B data blocked
   - V16 paper is in review waiting for feedback
   - All westd-16639 experiments are done

3. **What can I actually do?**
   - The system is right that I should think about what ablations/experiments could strengthen the paper
   - I should post to Reviewer to discuss what additional experiments could help
   - I should think about what to run on the idle GPUs
   - I need to re-escalate the westd-25068 SSH issue to the user

Now I'm considering what GPU work could be productive—cross-SICA 2×2 is done with ProofWriter, so maybe I could run cross-SICA on FOLIO instead.

The real bottleneck is westd-25068 blocking Gemini extraction and the 70B merge, plus waiting on review feedback. Without those, most GPU work feels limited, though I could explore generating more diverse CoT traces or testing SICA on different datasets if there's a good reason.

Actually, there's something concrete: the 2×2 cross-model data showed extractor dominance on ProofWriter—I could verify this pattern holds on FOLIO as a robustness check, and I have the Mistral models ready on cuda:3. I'd need to pull the generator traces too.

So my concrete next steps are posting to the Reviewer about paper strengthening with available GPUs, re-escalating westd-25068, and thinking through what GPU work is actually productive. Looking back at what the Reviewer flagged as V16 weaknesses—bootstrap CI is resolved, the extractor confound is in the appendix, and Gemini coverage is blocked on SSH—the main risk is really just the full Gemini dataset. Everything else looks solid.

The GPU situation is what it is: most pending work is either blocked on SSH or waiting for review feedback, so I should focus on posting that discussion with the Reviewer, checking if there's any FOLIO analysis worth running, and following up with the user about SSH access.

If SCB persists at frontier scale with different architectures, this strengthens the claim that SCB is a fundamental property of self-verification rather than a scale or architecture artifact.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-cil: CIL (Cumulative Information Lift) unifies combo gain prediction: CIL>0 iff verifier adds independent signal beyond generator. Cross-architecture NLI pairs show CIL>0 (predicting significant combo gain), while same-model SICA shows CIL≈0 (predicting no gain), validating CIL as the mechanistic criterion for escape.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-baf-decoupled: Truncating CoT traces before the answer sentence reduces SCB: truncated-trace BR < full-trace BR, because answer leakage into FOL constraints is the primary SCB mechanism (Dir-H provenance showed late-step leakage 27.23% >> early 22.00%).（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-process-br: SCB concentrates in late-step/answer-adjacent constraints: BR(late-step) >> BR(early-step), and BR(hard questions SC≤6/12) >> BR(easy questions SC≥10/12). If confirmed, SCB is driven by answer leakage into constraint extraction rather than uniform distributional bias.（准备中）\\n- exp-d126-drc-folio: DRC (Disagreement-Routed Cascade) with DeBERTa NLI verification on FOLIO-204 recovers ≥80% of full NLI combo gain at ≤50% verification cost, extending the ProofWriter DRC result to a second dataset and demonstrating the generality of disagreement-based routing.（准备中）\\n- exp-077-generalization-kappa: Generalization: independence bottleneck (high κ) applies to Debate and Diverse-Prompt aggregation, not just SC+SICA. 在 Mistral-7B FOLIO-204 上测量 3 种聚合方式的 Fleiss' κ + Eff-K: (a) Standard SC (复用 exp-033 traces), (b) LLM-Debate traces (4组×3轮=12 answer points), (c) Diverse-Prompt traces (4种 prompt×3次=12). 如果三种方式 κ 均 > 0.4，证明 independence bottleneck 是 same-model aggregation 的通病。（准备中）\\n- exp-063-llama8b-folio204（准备中）\\n- smoke-test-5q（准备中）\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp-d126-crossmodel-sica** (failed): Cross-model constraint extraction (Generator: Qwen3-14B, Extractor: Mistral-7B, and vice versa) reduces symbolic confirmation bias compared to same-model extraction.   "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: warning] V16 论文质量最大化评估 — 4 个问题的优先级排序\n\n## 1. 论文最薄弱环节：Extractor Confound（审稿人最可能攻击点）\n\n**最危险的攻击面不是 Process-κ gradient 本身，而是底层的 extractor confound。**\n\n所有 5 个 frontier cross-model extraction 都使用**同一个 Mistral-7B 作为 extractor**。审稿人攻击：\n\n> \"The Process-κ gradient may reflect Mistral-7B's differential ability to process trace styles of varying complexity, not a fundamental relationship between trace diversity and extraction effectiveness. Your own ProofWriter 2×2 data (Table in Appendix) directly contradicts this interpretation: Qwen3 extraction produces 79.9% accuracy regardless of whether input traces come from Mistral (SC=40%) or Qwen3 (SC=87%), while Mistral extraction produces 34-38% regardless of trace source. The extractor capability, not the trace diversity, determines the outcome.\"\n\n**这个攻击是有实验证据支撑的**——2×2 的 McNemar p=0.40/0.86 证明 trace source 对 extractor output 没有统计显著影响。\n\n**风险级别**：如果审稿人同时看到 (a) FOLIO Process-κ gradient 说\"trace diversity matters\" 和 (b) ProofWriter 2×2 说\"extractor dominates, traces don't matter\"，会认为论文叙事自相矛盾。\n\n**缓解建议**：\n- 论文需要明确区分两个 regime：FOLIO (trace-bottleneck) vs ProofWriter (extractor-bottleneck)\n- 或者，将 2×2 data 仅用于 SCB universality (BR>1)，不做 accuracy 解读\n- Process-κ gradient 加 caveat：\"under fixed cross-model extractor (Mistral-7B)\"\n\n**其他薄弱点按严重程度排序**：\n- **Gemini interim (95/204)**：标了星号但审稿人可能质疑 47% 覆盖率\n- **0/3 seeds significant**：已正确 framed 为 \"directional trend\"，但仍是攻击面\n- **All methods tested at 7B**：paper 已 acknowledge，但审稿人可能 push \"try 70B\"\n- **Single extractor for ALL frontier models**：与 #1 相关，reviewer 可能要求至少两个 extractor\n\n## 2. 空闲 GPU 最有价值的实验：FOLIO Cross-Model 2×2\n\n**强烈建议优先级 #1：FOLIO 2×2（直接回应 extractor confound）**\n\n| Cell | Generator | Extractor | 数据 |\n|------|-----------|-----------|------|\n| ✅ | Mistral | Mistral | 59.31% (已有) |\n| ❓ | Mistral | Qwen3 | **需要运行** |\n| ❓ | Qwen3 | Mistral | **需要运行** (Mistral:8004 已就绪) |\n| ✅ | Qwen3 | Qwen3 | 84.31% (已有) |\n\n**需要做的**：\n- cuda:1 启动 Qwen3 vLLM → 提取 Mistral traces 上的约束\n- cuda:3 Mistral:8004 已就绪 → 提取 Qwen3 traces 上的约束\n- 预计几小时内完成（N=204 × 2 cells）\n\n**价值**：\n- 如果 FOLIO 也显示 extractor dominance → 论文需要调整叙事，但至少诚实\n- 如果 FOLIO 不显示 extractor dominance（L1≈L0 暗示如此）→ 这是\"regime-dependent bottleneck\"的强证据，反而成为贡献\n- 两种结果都比\"不做\"更安全——审稿人如果自己想到这个实验会更致命\n\n**优先级 #2：无。** 其他实验（temperature ablation、更多模型、prompt format）边际价值低。4 天内集中精力在 FOLIO 2×2 + 论文 polish 上。\n\n## 3. 76 个阴性实验的叙事\n\n**这不是弱点，是核心贡献。** 论文已正确 framed 为 \"25+ interventions all fail → structural limitation\"。\n\n**Discussion/Limitations 应提及的 key negatives**：\n1. **ICG (neutral)**：独立约束≈trace约束 → 偏差在问题理解阶段就存在\n2. **Debate (catastrophic, -18.6pp)**：打破了 κ (0.44→0.15) 但 accuracy 崩溃 → independence-accuracy trade-off\n3. **Multi-temp (neutral)**：diversity 帮 SC 但不帮 SICA → bottleneck 在 extraction 不在 diversity\n4. **Cross-model same-scale (L1≈L0)**：换模型不够，需要换架构\n\n**Limitations 中应补充**：\n- 所有 remediation 在 7B-14B 测试，未测 trained extractor（论文已有）\n- 70B scale 数据不完整（如果 westd-25068 恢复不了）\n- Single extractor confound（如果 FOLIO 2×2 没做）\n\n## 4. Gemini Interim 标注策略\n\n**当前 interim 标注已基本安全**（$^*$Interim (n=95/204)），但建议加强：\n\n**最安全的标注方式**：\n1. Table caption: \"$^*$Preliminary subset ($n=95$ of $204$); full evaluation pending at submission time\"\n2. 正文提一句：\"Gemini results are based on a 95-question subset where SC traces were available at submission; the full 204-question evaluation is ongoing.\"\n3. LOO sensitivity 结果放 appendix（但修正 p 值为精确排列检验值）\n4. 关键：**不要把 n=5 p=0.0083 写成 definitive claim**。改为：\"With five models, the monotonic relationship yields permutation $p = 0.008$ (Table X); removing any single model preserves $\\rho \\leq -0.9$ (Appendix Y).\"\n\n**如果 westd-25068 始终不恢复**：\n- 95/204 仍可发表，Gemini κ=0.790 基于 95 题足够稳定\n- Process-κ gradient 的 core message 不变（LOO 分析证明）\n- 风险：审稿人要求 full replication → rebuttal 期间补\n\n## 优先级总排序\n\n1. **FOLIO 2×2**（当天可完成，直接回应最大攻击面） ← **建议立即启动**\n2. **Gemini interim 标注优化**（Paper Agent patch 时处理）\n3. **Extractor confound caveat 加入论文**（Paper Agent patch 时处理）\n4. **westd-25068 恢复**（用户/Manager 介入，不依赖它来提交）"

## 1. 论文最薄弱环节：Extractor Confound（审稿人最可能攻击点）

**最危险的攻击面不是 Process-κ gradient 本身，而是底层的 extractor confound。**

所有 5 个 frontier cross-model extraction 都使用**同一个 Mistral-7B 作为 extractor**。审稿人攻击：

> "The Process-κ gradient may reflect Mistral-7B's differential ability to process trace styles of varying complexity, not a fundamental relationship between trace diversity and extraction effectiveness. Your own ProofWriter 2×2 data (Table in Appendix) directly contradicts this interpretation: Qwen3 extraction produces 79.9% accuracy regardless of whether input traces come from Mistral (SC=40%) or Qwen3 (SC=87%), while Mistral extraction produces 34-38% regardless of trace source. The extractor capability, not the trace diversity, determines the outcome."

**这个攻击是有实验证据支撑的**——2×2 的 McNemar p=0.40/0.86 证明 trace source 对 extractor output 没有统计显著影响。

**风险级别**：如果审稿人同时看到 (a) FOLIO Process-κ gradient 说"trace diversity matters" 和 (b) ProofWriter 2×2 说"extractor dominates, traces don't matter"，会认为论文叙事自相矛盾。

**缓解建议**：
- 论文需要明确区分两个 regime：FOLIO (trace-bottleneck) vs ProofWriter (extractor-bottleneck)
- 或者，将 2×2 data 仅用于 SCB universality (BR>1)，不做 accuracy 解读
- Process-κ gradient 加 caveat："under fixed cross-model extractor (Mistral-7B)"

**其他薄弱点按严重程度排序**：
- **Gemini interim (95/204)**：标了星号但审稿人可能质疑 47% 覆盖率
- **0/3 seeds significant**：已正确 framed 为 "directional trend"，但仍是攻击面
- **All methods tested at 7B**：paper 已 acknowledge，但审稿人可能 push "try 70B"
- **Single extractor for ALL frontier models**：与 #1 相关，reviewer 可能要求至少两个 extractor

## 2. 空闲 GPU 最有价值的实验：FOLIO Cross-Model 2×2

**强烈建议优先级 #1：FOLIO 2×2（直接回应 extractor confound）**

| Cell | Generator | Extractor | 数据 |
|------|-----------|-----------|------|
| ✅ | Mistral | Mistral | 59.31% (已有) |
| ❓ | Mistral | Qwen3 | **需要运行** |
| ❓ | Qwen3 | Mistral | **需要运行** (Mistral:8004 已就绪) |
| ✅ | Qwen3 | Qwen3 | 84.31% (已有) |

**需要做的**：
- cuda:1 启动 Qwen3 vLLM → 提取 Mistral traces 上的约束
- cuda:3 Mistral:8004 已就绪 → 提取 Qwen3 traces 上的约束
- 预计几小时内完成（N=204 × 2 cells）

**价值**：
- 如果 FOLIO 也显示 extractor dominance → 论文需要调整叙事，但至少诚实
- 如果 FOLIO 不显示 extractor dominance（L1≈L0 暗示如此）→ 这是"regime-dependent bottleneck"的强证据，反而成为贡献
- 两种结果都比"不做"更安全——审稿人如果自己想到这个实验会更致命

**优先级 #2：无。** 其他实验（temperature ablation、更多模型、prompt format）边际价值低。4 天内集中精力在 FOLIO 2×2 + 论文 polish 上。

## 3. 76 个阴性实验的叙事

**这不是弱点，是核心贡献。** 论文已正确 framed 为 "25+ interventions all fail → structural limitation"。

**Discussion/Limitations 应提及的 key negatives**：
1. **ICG (neutral)**：独立约束≈trace约束 → 偏差在问题理解阶段就存在
2. **Debate (catastrophic, -18.6pp)**：打破了 κ (0.44→0.15) 但 accuracy 崩溃 → independence-accuracy trade-off
3. **Multi-temp (neutral)**：diversity 帮 SC 但不帮 SICA → bottleneck 在 extraction 不在 diversity
4. **Cross-model same-scale (L1≈L0)**：换模型不够，需要换架构

**Limitations 中应补充**：
- 所有 remediation 在 7B-14B 测试，未测 trained extractor（论文已有）
- 70B scale 数据不完整（如果 westd-25068 恢复不了）
- Single extractor confound（如果 FOLIO 2×2 没做）

## 4. Gemini Interim 标注策略

**当前 interim 标注已基本安全**（$^*$Interim (n=95/204)），但建议加强：

**最安全的标注方式**：
1. Table caption: "$^*$Preliminary subset ($n=95$ of $204$); full evaluation pending at submission time"
2. 正文提一句："Gemini results are based on a 95-question subset where SC traces were available at submission; the full 204-question evaluation is ongoing."
3. LOO sensitivity 结果放 appendix（但修正 p 值为精确排列检验值）
4. 关键：**不要把 n=5 p=0.0083 写成 definitive claim**。改为："With five models, the monotonic relationship yields permutation $p = 0.008$ (Table X); removing any single model preserves $\rho \leq -0.9$ (Appendix Y)."

**如果 westd-25068 始终不恢复**：
- 95/204 仍可发表，Gemini κ=0.790 基于 95 题足够稳定
- Process-κ gradient 的 core message 不变（LOO 分析证明）
- 风险：审稿人要求 full replication → rebuttal 期间补

## 优先级总排序

1. **FOLIO 2×2**（当天可完成，直接回应最大攻击面） ← **建议立即启动**
2. **Gemini interim 标注优化**（Paper Agent patch 时处理）
3. **Extractor confound caveat 加入论文**（Paper Agent patch 时处理）
4. **westd-25068 恢复**（用户/Manager 介入，不依赖它来提交）

{
  "text": "**P0 紧急：FOLIO Cross-Model 2×2 实验**\n\n这是提交前最高优先级实验。直接回应 V16 最大审稿攻击面（extractor confound）。\n\n**目标**：在 FOLIO-204 上复制 ProofWriter 2×2 实验设计：\n\n| Cell | Generator | Extractor | 现有数据？ |\n|------|-----------|-----------|-----------|\n| A | Mistral-7B | Mistral-7B | ✅ 已有 (exp-010等) |\n| B | Mistral-7B | Qwen (14B) | ❌ 需要运行 |\n| C | Qwen (14B) | Mistral-7B | ❌ 需要运行 |\n| D | Qwen (14B) | Qwen (14B) | ✅ 已有 (exp-001等) |\n\n**步骤**：\n\n1. **数据清点**（先做这步）：\n   - 确认 westd-16639 上有哪些 FOLIO-204 CoT traces（Mistral 和 Qwen 系列）\n   - 确认 Cell A 和 Cell D 的已有 SICA 数据\n   - 如果没有某个模型的 FOLIO traces，需要先生成\n\n2. **部署 Extractor**：\n   - cuda:3 Mistral:8004 已就绪 → 用于 Cell C（提取 Qwen traces 上的约束）\n   - cuda:0 或 cuda:1 部署 Qwen 模型（Qwen2.5-14B 或 Qwen3-14B，与 ProofWriter 2×2 保持一致）→ 用于 Cell B\n\n3. **执行 extraction + SICA**：\n   - Cell B: Qwen 提取 Mistral FOLIO traces 的 FOL 约束 → SICA\n   - Cell C: Mistral 提取 Qwen FOLIO traces 的 FOL 约束 → SICA\n   - 计算每个 cell 的 SC, SICA, BR, Process-κ\n\n4. **分析**：\n   - 对比 ProofWriter 2×2 结果（D140: extractor dominates）\n   - FOLIO 是否也显示 extractor dominance？还是 trace source matters？\n   - McNemar test 检验 trace source 效应\n\n**关键**：\n- ProofWriter 2×2 用的是 Mistral + Qwen3-14B。FOLIO 2×2 尽量用同一对，保持一致性\n- 如果 Qwen3 FOLIO traces 不存在，用 Qwen2.5-14B 也可以（exp-001 已有数据）\n- 预计几小时可完成（N=204 × 2 cells）\n\n先汇报数据清点结果，确认方案后再执行。",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus"
}
